## Section 29: Performance, memory, responsiveness, and scalability
<a id="section-29-performance-memory-responsiveness-and-scalability"></a>

### Q422: Verify cold startup performs no avoidable heavy work on the main thread

Review the current SquareTool checkout for the following task: **Verify cold startup performs no avoidable heavy work on the main thread**.
